This prevents duplicate work and wasted context.\n277\t\n278\tContinue to collect_confirmations.\n279\t</step>\n280\t\n281\t<step name=\"collect_confirmations\">\n282\tWait for all 4 background agents to finish, then read each agent's output file to collect confirmations.\n283\t\n284\tEach `Agent(...)` call above with `run_in_background=true` returns an `async_launched` result that carries an `outputFile` path (and `canReadOutputFile: true`). The 4 agents run concurrently and each one's completion arrives as a message in this conversation when it finishes — do NOT issue a separate blocking call to wait for them.\n285\t\n286\t**Once all 4 agents have reported completion, read each agent's output file (single message with 4 Read calls):**\n287\t```\n288\tRead tool:\n289\t  file_path: \"{outputFile from that agent's async_launched result}\"\n290\t```\n291\t\n292\t> Allow up to `workflow.subagent_timeout` for the slowest agent to finish before treating it as failed. The timeout is configurable via `workflow.subagent_timeout` in `.planning/config.json` (milliseconds). Default: 300000 (5 minutes). Increase for large codebases or slower models.\n293\t\n294\tEach output file contains that agent's completion confirmation. Parse the confirmation marker (see below) from the file contents.\n295\t\n296\t**Expected confirmation format from each agent:**\n297\t```\n298\t## Mapping Complete\n299\t\n300\t**Focus:** {focus}\n301\t**Documents written:**\n302\t- `.planning/codebase/{DOC1}.md` ({N} lines)\n303\t- `.planning/codebase/{DOC2}.md` ({N} lines)\n304\t\n305\tReady for orchestrator summary.\n306\t```\n307\t\n308\t**What you receive:** Just file paths and line counts. NOT document contents.\n309\t\n310\tIf any agent failed, note the failure and continue with successful documents.\n311\t\n312\tContinue to verify_output.\n313\t</step>\n314\t\n315\t<step name=\"sequential_mapping\" condition=\"Agent tool is NOT available (e.g. Antigravity, Codex)\">\n316\tWhen the `Agent` tool is unavailable, perform codebase mapping sequentially in the current context. This replaces `spawn_agents` and `collect_confirmations`.\n317\t\n318\t**IMPORTANT:** Do NOT use `browser_subagent`, `Explore`, or any browser-based tool. Use only file system tools (Read, Bash, Write, Grep, Glob, list_dir, view_file, grep_search, or equivalent tools available in your runtime).\n319\t\n320\t**IMPORTANT:** Set all date stamps (`**Analysis Date:**`, footer, `<!-- refreshed -->`) to `{date}` from init context, overwriting any existing date — Update runs seed from files with concrete prior dates, so merely replacing `[YYYY-MM-DD]` placeholders is not sufficient. NEVER guess the date.\n321\t\n322\t**SCOPE:** When `${PATH_SCOPE_HINT}` is non-empty (i.e. `--paths` was supplied), restrict every pass below to the validated path prefixes in `${SCOPED_PATHS}`. Include actual file paths formatted with backticks.\n346\t\n347\tContinue to verify_output.\n348\t</step>\n349\t\n350\t<step name=\"verify_output\">\n351\tVerify all documents created successfully:\n352\t\n353\t```bash\n354\tls -la .planning/codebase/\n355\twc -l .planning/codebase/*.md\n356\t```\n357\t\n358\t**Verification checklist:**\n359\t- All 7 documents exist\n360\t- No empty documents (each should have >20 lines)\n361\t\n362\tIf any documents missing or empty, note which agents may have failed.\n363\t\n364\tContinue to stamp_codebase_map.\n365\t</step>\n366\t\n367\t<step name=\"stamp_codebase_map\">\n368\tStamp the drift baseline into every document that was just written:\n369\t\n370\t```bash\n371\tgsd_run stamp-codebase-map ${UPDATED_DOCS:+--files \"$UPDATED_DOCS\"}\n372\t```\n373\t\n374\tThis writes `last_mapped_commit: <HEAD sha>` and `last_mapped_at: <date>` into\n375\tthe YAML frontmatter of each `.planning/codebase/*.md` that exists. It runs on\n376\tevery mapping run, incremental (`--paths`) and full alike. `--files` narrows it\n377\tto the documents an Update run actually refreshed; `--paths` needs no narrowing\n378\tbecause all seven are regenerated, just scoped in content.\n379\t\n380\t**Why this is a shell step and not an instruction to the mapper.** The stamp is\n381\tthe only machine-readable freshness marker: the `verify codebase-drift` gate\n382\treads it to decide what to diff HEAD against. The human-readable markers the\n383\tmapper writes (`**Analysis Date:**`, `<!-- refreshed: ... -->`) are restamped\n384\tunconditionally on an Update run, so a mapper that decides its work is already\n385\tdone and rewrites only the dates still looks fresh to a human. Leaving the\n386\tmachine-readable stamp to the same agent reproduces exactly the failure the\n387\tstamp exists to detect. A shell step cannot be skipped by a confident agent.\n388\t\n389\tThe command is non-blocking: it emits `skipped` with a `reason` outside a git\n390\trepo or when no documents exist. Report `stamped` and `commit` in the summary\n391\tif any entry in `failed` is non-empty; otherwise continue silently.\n392\t\n393\tRun in this position, before `commit_codebase_map`, the stamp lands on\n394\tdocuments the mapper just wrote, so its markdown whitespace normalization is\n395\tfolded into the same commit. Running `stamp-codebase-map` by hand against an\n396\talready-committed map reflows that map's whitespace as a side effect.\n397\t\n398\tContinue to scan_for_secrets.\n399\t</step>\n400\t\n401\t<step name=\"scan_for_secrets\">\n402\t**CRITICAL SECURITY CHECK:** Scan output files for accidentally leaked secrets before committing.\n403\t\n404\tRun secret pattern detection:\n405\t\n406\t```bash\n407\t# Check for common API key patterns in generated docs\n408\tgrep -E '(sk-[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|sk_live_[a-zA-Z0-9]+|sk_test_[a-zA-Z0-9]+|ghp_[a-zA-Z0-9]{36}|gho_[a-zA-Z0-9]{36}|glpat-[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+|AKIA[A-Z0-9]{16}|xox[baprs]-[a-zA-Z0-9-]+|[REDACTED:gsd-scan]|eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\\.eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\\.)' .planning/codebase/*.md 2>/dev/null && SECRETS_FOUND=true || SECRETS_FOUND=false\n```\n\n**If SECRETS_FOUND=true:**\n\n```\n⚠️  SECURITY ALERT: Potential secrets detected in codebase documents!\n\nFound patterns that look like API keys or tokens in:\n[show grep output]\n\nThis would expose credentials if committed.\n\n**Action required:**\n1. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01MCPx4Cb4JLRDZLjbDkWZpZ","type":"tool_result","content":"1\texport type Theme = 'light' | 'dark'\n2\t\n3\texport const THEME_STORAGE_KEY = 'theme'\n4\t\n5\t/** The current theme, read from html[data-theme]. Only 'dark' counts as dark. */\n6\texport function readTheme(): Theme {\n7\t  return document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light'\n8\t}\n9\t\n10\t/**\n11\t * True when the visitor chose a theme but storage refused it. Storage is the record of an\n12\t * explicit choice, so without this flag a blocked-storage visitor would look like one who\n13\t * never chose, and the next OS scheme change would silently undo their choice.\n14\t */\n15\tlet unsavedChoice = false\n16\t\n17\t/**\n18\t * Switch the theme: the attribute is written first so the switch always works, then the\n19\t * choice is stored. When storage is blocked the choice lasts for the page session only, and\n20\t * the OS scheme no longer overrides it.\n21\t */\n22\texport function applyTheme(theme: Theme): void {\n23\t  document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', theme)\n24\t  try {\n25\t    localStorage.setItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY, theme)\n26\t    unsavedChoice = false\n27\t  } catch {\n28\t    // Storage blocked (private mode, policy): the in-memory choice still applies.\n29\t    unsavedChoice = true\n30\t  }\n31\t}\n32\t\n33\tconst DARK_QUERY = '(prefers-color-scheme: dark)'\n34\t\n35\tfunction isTheme(value: unknown): value is Theme {\n36\t  return value === 'light' || value === 'dark'\n37\t}\n38\t\n39\tfunction storedTheme(): Theme | null {\n40\t  try {\n41\t    const value = localStorage.getItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY)\n42\t    return isTheme(value) ? value : null\n43\t  } catch {\n44\t    return null\n45\t  }\n46\t}\n47\t\n48\tfunction systemTheme(): Theme {\n49\t  try {\n50\t    return window.matchMedia(DARK_QUERY).matches ? Both are folded into\n66\t * html[data-theme], which stays the single source of truth, and `onChange` fires when that\n67\t * attribute changes for any reason. It has no backend API, no database, no authentication, no analytics, and no form submission endpoint. The `src/` tree contains no `fetch`, `XMLHttpRequest`, or `sendBeacon` calls. External contact is limited to outbound links, `mailto:`, the Clipboard API, and browser storage.\n\n## APIs & External Services\n\n**Outbound links only (no SDK, no API calls):**\n- GitHub profile - link defined in `src/content/contacts.ts` (kind `github`, `external: true`)\n- LinkedIn profile - link defined in `src/content/contacts.ts` (kind `linkedin`, `external: true`)\n- Instagram profile - link defined in `src/content/contacts.ts` (kind `instagram`, `external: true`, custom `ariaLabel`)\n- GitHub repository for the RecipeCart project - `links.code` in `src/content/projects.ts`\n- Icons for these links are inline SVG components in `src/components/ui/icons/` (`GitHubIcon.tsx`, `LinkedInIcon.tsx`, `InstagramIcon.tsx`), not an icon-font or CDN\n\n**Email:**\n- Email is a `mailto:` link. The source of truth is the `kind: 'email'` entry in `src/content/contacts.ts`. `src/app/Page.tsx` strips the `mailto:` prefix to get the address for `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx` and `src/sections/Contact/Contact.tsx`\n- The same `mailto:` address is also hard-coded in the static shell `index.html` (Hero \"Copy email\" anchor), so changing the address requires editing both `src/content/contacts.ts` and `index.html`\n- Copy-to-clipboard uses `navigator.clipboard.writeText` in `src/lib/copy.ts`; `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts` wraps it. On failure, `openMail()` in `src/lib/copy.ts` falls back to opening the `mailto:` link\n\n**Static file download:**\n- Resume is served from `public/resume.pdf` and linked with `import.meta.env.BASE_URL` in `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`, `src/sections/Contact/Contact.tsx`, `src/layout/SiteHeader.tsx`, `src/layout/PillNav.tsx`. There is no external document service\n\n## Data Storage\n\n**Databases:**\n- Not applicable. No database client or ORM is present in `package.json`\n\n**File Storage:**\n- Local filesystem only, at build time. Images are imported from `src/assets/images/` and transformed by `vite-imagetools` (see `src/content/images.ts`). Output is bundled into `dist/`\n- Static files under `public/` (`resume.pdf`, `favicon.svg`) are copied as-is\n\n**Browser storage (client-side only):**\n- `localStorage` key `theme` (constant `THEME_STORAGE_KEY` in `src/lib/theme.ts`) records an explicit light/dark choice\n- The same key is read before first paint by the inline script in `index.html` and `404.html`, so the two must stay in sync with `src/lib/theme.ts`\n- If storage is blocked, `applyTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts` catches the error and keeps the choice in memory for the page session only\n\n**Caching:**\n- None at the application level. Caching is whatever GitHub Pages applies to the static assets\n\n## Authentication & Identity\n\n**Auth Provider:**\n- None. The site has no user accounts or protected routes\n\n**Browser-level inputs read for behaviour (not identity):**\n- `prefers-color-scheme` media query, read in `src/lib/theme.ts` and the inline script in `index.html`\n- `prefers-reduced-motion`, read by `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts` and fed to both animation libraries in `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`\n- `storage` events in other tabs, subscribed in `subscribeTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts`\n\n## Monitoring & Observability\n\n**Error Tracking:**\n- None detected\n\n**Analytics:**\n- None detected. No analytics script or tag appears in `index.html`, `404.html`, or `src/`\n\n**Logs:**\n- No runtime logging. Live-page smoke check only: `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` fetches the deployed page URL with `curl` and greps for the site owner name after deployment\n\n## CI/CD & Deployment\n\n**Hosting:**\n- GitHub Pages. Deployed by `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` using `actions/configure-pages`, `actions/upload-pages-artifact`, and `actions/deploy-pages`. All actions are pinned to commit SHAs\n- Pages environment `github-pages`; the deploy job requests `pages: write` and `id-token: write`\n\n**CI Pipeline:**\n- GitHub Actions\n  - `.github/workflows/ci.yml` - on every `push` and `pull_request`: `npm ci`, `npm run verify`, `npx playwright install --with-deps chromium`, `npm run test:e2e`\n  - `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` - on push to `main` (and `workflow_dispatch`): build job runs `npm run verify` and uploads `./dist`; e2e job runs browser tests; deploy job publishes to Pages only if both succeed\n- Playwright downloads Chromium at CI time from Playwright's distribution. This is a build-time dependency only and is not part of the shipped bundle\n\n## Environment Configuration\n\n**Required env vars:**\n- None required to run locally\n- `VITE_BASE` (optional) - base path for asset URLs. CI sets it to `/aa-phan-portfolio-v2/` in both workflows; default in `vite.config.ts` is `/`\n- Read in `vite.config.ts` through `loadEnv(mode, process.cwd(), '')`, which also includes process environment\n\n**Secrets location:**\n- No application secrets. The workflows reference no repository secret values; the Pages deploy authenticates through GitHub OIDC (`id-token: write`) rather than a stored token\n- No credential or `.env` file contents were read for this map\n\n## Webhooks & Callbacks\n\n**Incoming:**\n- None. No server endpoints exist in the repo\n\n**Outgoing:**\n- None. Overview\n\n```text\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                 Static HTML shells (build entries)               │\n│     `index.html` (main)                 `404.html` (notFound)    │\n│   inline theme bootstrap, static first-paint markup              │\n└───────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────┘\n                            │ <script type=\"module\" src=\"/src/main.tsx\">\n                            ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  Bootstrap: `src/main.tsx` → `src/app/App.tsx`                   │\n└───────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────┘\n                            ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  Providers: `src/app/Providers.tsx`                              │\n│  MotionPreferenceProvider → MotionFeatures → ToastProvider       │\n└───────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────┘\n                            ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  Page composition: `src/app/Page.tsx`                            │\n│  SiteHeader · <main> sections · Footer · PillNav                 │\n└──────┬──────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────┘\n       │ reads                        │ reads\n       ▼                              ▼\n┌──────────────────────────┐   ┌────────────────────────────────┐\n│ Content (typed data)     │   │ Hooks / stores                  │\n│ `src/content/*.ts`       │   │ `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` │\n│ via `src/content/index`  │   │ `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`         │\n│                          │   │ `src/lib/theme.ts`              │\n└──────────────────────────┘   └────────────────────────────────┘\n       │ props\n       ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  Sections: `src/sections/{Hero,Experience,Projects,About,        │\n│            Skills,Education,Contact}/`                           │\n│  Shared chrome: `src/layout/`   Primitives: `src/components/ui/` │\n│  Motion: `src/anim/`            Styles: `src/styles/`            │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n## Component Responsibilities\n\n| Component | Responsibility | File |\n|-----------|----------------|------|\n| Bootstrap | Mount React root in StrictMode, load font and global CSS | `src/main.tsx` |\n| App | Wrap `Page` in `Providers` | `src/app/App.tsx` |\n| Providers | Compose motion preference, motion feature loading, and toast context | `src/app/Providers.tsx` |\n| Page | Derive email and other links from content; choose which sections render, in order | `src/app/Page.tsx` |\n| Content index | Single export object `siteContent` for all typed site data | `src/content/index.ts` |\n| Content schema | TypeScript types for every content record (`Profile`, `Project`, `ContactLink`, `ImageAsset`, ...) | `src/content/schema.ts` |\n| Scroll-spy | Pick the active section for nav highlighting; lock indicator during click scroll; deep-link jump | `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` |\n| Theme store | Read/write `html[data-theme]`, persist choice, follow OS and other tabs | `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/hooks/useTheme.ts` |\n| Toast | One toast at a time, polite live region, 4 s auto-hide | `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `src/components/ui/useToast.ts` |\n| Motion layer | Reduced-motion preference, feature loading, springs and easing tokens | `src/anim/index.ts`, `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `src/anim/tokens.ts` |\n| Layout chrome | Header, floating pill nav, footer, skip link, section frame | `src/layout/` |\n| Project crate and dialog | Horizontal snap row of project sleeves; native `<dialog>` for details | `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`, `src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx` |\n| Build-time gates | Budgets, colour and contrast, path case, phone-number check, image check | `scripts/*.mjs` |\n\n## Pattern Overview\n\n**Overall:** Single-page static React application. Typed, in-repo content feeds a composition root; there is no router, no server, and no runtime data fetching.\n\n**Key Characteristics:**\n- Content is code: every piece of copy, project, and link is a typed TypeScript module in `src/content/`, validated by `satisfies` clauses (for example `src/content/contacts.ts` uses `as const satisfies readonly ContactLink[]`)\n- One page with anchor sections (`SECTION_IDS` in `src/layout/nav-items.ts`); section order is fixed in `src/app/Page.tsx`\n- Theme state lives on the DOM (`html[data-theme]`), not in React state. Components subscribe with `useSyncExternalStore` through `useTheme` in `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`\n- Two animation systems are configured from one preference source. `MotionConfig` (motion) and `Globals.assign` (react-spring) both read `useMotionPreference`\n- Styling is Tailwind utilities plus named semantic layers in `src/styles/` (for example `band-invert` for the inverted Projects band, `cta` classes in `interactions.css`)\n\n## Layers\n\n**Shell (static HTML):**\n- Purpose: First paint, theme bootstrap before React, and the not-found page\n- Location: `index.html`, `404.html`\n- Contains: meta tags, inline `<script>` that sets `data-theme` from `localStorage` or `prefers-color-scheme`, static copy for the hero and section headings\n- Depends on: `public/favicon.svg`, `public/resume.pdf` via `%BASE_URL%`\n- Used by: Vite build (both are rollup inputs in `vite.config.ts`); GitHub Pages serves `404.html` for unknown paths\n\n**App composition:**\n- Purpose: Mount React and order the page\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`, `src/app/`\n- Contains: `App`, `Providers`, `Page`\n- Depends on: `src/content/index.ts`, `src/anim/index.ts`, `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`, `src/layout/*`, `src/sections/*`\n- Used by: Nothing else; it is the root\n\n**Content:**\n- Purpose: The site's data as typed modules\n- Location: `src/content/`\n- Contains: `profile.ts`, `experience.ts`, `projects.ts`, `skills.ts`, `education.ts`, `contacts.ts`, `images.ts`, `schema.ts`, `index.ts`\n- Depends on: `src/assets/images/` (via `images.ts`), `schema.ts` types\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx` and, through props, the sections\n\n**Sections:**\n- Purpose: One feature per page section, each receiving its data as props\n- Location: `src/sections/{Hero,Experience,Projects,About,Skills,Education,Contact}/`\n- Contains: `*.tsx` components; `Projects/` also has `Sleeve.tsx`, `ProjectDialog.tsx`, `project-types.ts`\n- Depends on: `src/layout/Section.tsx`, `src/components/ui/*`, `src/anim/*`, `src/lib/*`, `src/content/*` types\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`\n\n**Layout chrome:**\n- Purpose: Persistent navigation and page frame\n- Location: `src/layout/`\n- Contains: `SiteHeader.tsx` (desktop nav), `PillNav.tsx` (phone nav and pill), `Footer.tsx`, `Section.tsx`, `Container.tsx`, `SkipLink.tsx`, `Wordmark.tsx`, `nav-items.ts`\n- Depends on: `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` (passed `activeId` and `onNavigate` as props from `Page`), `src/anim/*`\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`, sections\n\n**Hooks and stores:**\n- Purpose: Shared behaviour outside React rendering\n- Location: `src/hooks/`, `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`\n- Contains: `useActiveSection`, `useTheme`, theme read/write/subscribe functions\n- Depends on: DOM APIs (`IntersectionObserver`, `MutationObserver`, `matchMedia`, `localStorage`)\n- Used by: `Page.tsx`, `ThemeToggle`, `useCopyEmail`\n\n**UI primitives:**\n- Purpose: Reusable, low-level controls and icons\n- Location: `src/components/ui/`, `src/components/motion-primitives/`\n- Contains: `ThemeToggle.tsx`, `CtaLink.tsx`, `Toast.tsx`, `useToast.ts`, `useCopyEmail.ts`, `icons/*`, `text-effect.tsx`\n- Depends on: `src/anim/*`, `src/lib/*`\n- Used by: sections and layout\n\n**Animation layer:**\n- Purpose: Motion components, feature loading, and tokens\n- Location: `src/anim/`\n- Contains: `MotionFeatures.tsx` and `featuresStatus.ts` (feature loading state), `MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `useMotionPreference.ts`, `tokens.ts`, `declarative/` (`TextReveal`, `ToastPresence`, `NavIndicator`, `PillMorph`), `physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`, `features.ts`\n- Depends on: `motion/react`, `@react-spring/web`\n- Used by: `Providers`, layout, sections, UI primitives\n\n**Utilities:**\n- Purpose: Pure helpers\n- Location: `src/lib/`\n- Contains: `utils.ts` (`cn`), `copy.ts` (clipboard and mail fallback), `theme.ts`\n\n## Data Flow\n\n### Primary Request Path (first load)\n\n1. Browser requests the built `index.html`. The inline script sets `html[data-theme]` before any paint (`index.html`)\n2. Module `src/main.tsx` imports the font CSS and `src/styles/index.css`, then calls `createRoot(...).render(<StrictMode><App/></StrictMode>)`\n3. `App` renders `Providers`, then `Page` (`src/app/App.tsx`)\n4. `Page` reads `siteContent` from `src/content/index.ts` and computes `email` and `otherLinks` from `contacts` (`src/app/Page.tsx`)\n5. `Page` calls `useActiveSection(SECTION_IDS)`; the hook also performs a one-time jump for `#section` deep links (`src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`)\n6. `Page` renders sections in fixed order, passing content as props, then `Footer` and `PillNav`\n\n### Scroll and Navigation\n\n1. An `IntersectionObserver` watches each section element with a band below the sticky nav (`src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`)\n2. `pickActive` chooses the last section in page order that intersects the band; at page bottom the last section wins\n3. Clicking a nav item calls `lockTo(id)`, which sets the active id immediately and suspends observer updates until `scrollend` or a fallback timer\n4. `SiteHeader` and `PillNav` receive `activeId` and `onNavigate` as props from `Page`\n\n### Theme Toggle\n\n1. `ThemeToggle` calls `useTheme().toggle` (`src/hooks/useTheme.ts`)\n2. `applyTheme` writes `html[data-theme]` first, then persists to `localStorage` key `theme` (`src/lib/theme.ts`)\n3. `useSyncExternalStore` re-reads the attribute via `readTheme`; `MutationObserver` in `subscribeTheme` also catches attribute changes from other sources\n4. Other tabs: `storage` events re-sync the attribute. OS scheme changes apply only when nothing is stored\n\n### Project Dialog\n\n1. `Projects` holds `openIndex` state and passes `onOpen` to each `Sleeve` (`src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`)\n2. `ProjectDialog` renders a native `<dialog>` after the inverted section, so it keeps the page theme (`src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx`)\n\n**State Management:**\n- Local component state with `useState` for dialog and toast state\n- DOM as state for theme (`html[data-theme]`)\n- Static content is module-level constants, not state\n\n## Key Abstractions\n\n**Content record types:**\n- Purpose: Typed shape for each content domain\n- Examples: `src/content/schema.ts` (`Profile`, `Project`, `ExperienceEntry`, `EducationEntry`, `SkillGroup`, `ContactLink`, `ImageAsset`, `DateRange`, `Writeup`)\n- Pattern: Interfaces in `schema.ts`; data files use `satisfies` to check conformance\n\n**ImageAsset:**\n- Purpose: Image metadata plus AVIF and WebP sources for `<picture>`\n- Examples: `src/content/images.ts` (`aboutPhoto`, `capitalOneLogo`, `spectrumLogo`, `symboticLogo`)\n- Pattern: Built from `vite-imagetools` imports with `?w=...&format=avif;webp&as=picture`\n\n**Section frame:**\n- Purpose: Consistent heading, spacing, and optional aside for each section\n- Examples: `src/layout/Section.tsx`, used by `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`\n- Pattern: Section components pass `id`, `title`, `mood`, `className`, and `aside` props\n\n**Toast context:**\n- Purpose: Let any component show a short message\n- Examples: `src/components/ui/useToast.ts` (context and `useToast`), `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx` (provider)\n- Pattern: Context with a stable `show(message)` function; default context is a no-op\n\n**Theme store:**\n- Purpose: One source of truth for light or dark\n- Examples: `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`\n- Pattern: External store on `html[data-theme]`, consumed with `useSyncExternalStore`\n\n## Entry Points\n\n**Application:**\n- Location: `index.html` loads `/src/main.tsx`\n- Triggers: Any navigation to the site root or a deep link such as `#projects`\n- Responsibilities: Theme bootstrap, React mount\n\n**Not-found page:**\n- Location: `404.html` (second Vite input)\n- Triggers: GitHub Pages serves it for unknown paths\n- Responsibilities: Static \"not part of this site\" page with the same theme bootstrap\n\n**Dev and build:**\n- `vite` (dev server) and `vite build` (`dist/`), configured in `vite.config.ts`\n- Vitest entry: `vitest.config.ts` runs `tests/unit/**`; Playwright entry: `playwright.config.ts` runs `tests/e2e/**`\n\n## Architectural Constraints\n\n- **Threading:** Single-threaded browser main thread. No Web Workers in use\n- **Global state:** Module-level `unsavedChoice` flag in `src/lib/theme.ts`; module-level `loaded` in `src/anim/featuresStatus.ts`; global react-spring `Globals` mutated by `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`\n- **Circular imports:** None detected in the paths reviewed\n- **Base path:** Every asset URL must go through `import.meta.env.BASE_URL` or `%BASE_URL%`. `vite.config.ts` appends a trailing slash to `VITE_BASE` because Vite does not add one\n- **Duplicated bootstrap:** The theme inline script is copied in `index.html` and `404.html`. The storage key `'theme'` is a literal there and a constant in `src/lib/theme.ts`\n- **rootMargin in JS:** `useActiveSection` builds `rootMargin` in code because CSS `var()` and `calc()` are not accepted by IntersectionObserver\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n### Hard-coded contact details in the static shell\n\n**What happens:** `index.html` and `404.html` contain literal copy, including the `mailto:` address and resume path in the Hero, separately from `src/content/*`.\n**Why it's wrong:** Changing `src/content/contacts.ts` or `src/content/profile.ts` does not update the first-paint markup, so the static HTML and the React page can disagree until hydration replaces the root.\n**Do this instead:** Keep copy changes in `src/content/`, and when the static shell must match, update `index.html` in the same change. `tests/unit/hero-parity.test.tsx` exists to check hero parity; extend that test rather than adding a new copy source.\n\n### Email derived by prefix slicing\n\n**What happens:** `src/app/Page.tsx` finds the `kind: 'email'` contact and strips the `mailto:` prefix with `slice(MAILTO.length)`.\n**Why it's wrong:** The address format is encoded in two places: the content `href` and the slice length. A contact with a different scheme would produce a wrong address silently.\n**Do this instead:** Store the address as a separate content field or derive it with a parser that checks the scheme before use.\n\n## Error Handling\n\n**Strategy:** Defensive, non-throwing browser API use. Storage and media-query calls are wrapped in `try/catch` and fall back to in-memory or default behaviour.\n\n**Patterns:**\n- `applyTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts` keeps the choice in memory when `localStorage` throws\n- `readTheme` / `systemTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts` return `'light'` when `matchMedia` is unavailable\n- `hashTarget` in `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` returns `null` for undecodable or unknown hashes\n- `copyEmail` in `src/lib/copy.ts` falls back to `openMail` on failure, with a toast message\n- No error boundary was found in `src/`\n\n## Cross-Cutting Concerns\n\n**Logging:** None in application code (no `console` calls were located in `src/`)\n**Validation:** Compile-time only, via TypeScript types and `satisfies` in `src/content/`; build-time checks in `scripts/*.mjs`\n**Authentication:** Not applicable\n\n---\n\n*Architecture analysis: 2026-11-19*\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3140,"cache_read_input_tokens":85081,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3140,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_014u2UY2M6z3XSuqGC4empsM","agent_id":"a96f7ac8c76e47557","session_id":"98e63eee-0876-4645-936f-e3b5a8ce91b3","uuid":"2b703957-c0f2-4158-a784-6fac05df7fb0","timestamp":"2026-11-20T03:28:03.807Z","request_id":"req_011ChDUA7K1n2Xg7hQ7yDh7g","subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Scan codebase tech and arch"}

Overview\n\n```text\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                 Static HTML shells (build entries)               │\n│     `index.html` (main)                 `404.html` (notFound)    │\n│   inline theme bootstrap, static first-paint markup              │\n└───────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────┘\n                            │ <script type=\"module\" src=\"/src/main.tsx\">\n                            ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  Bootstrap: `src/main.tsx` → `src/app/App.tsx`                   │\n└───────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────┘\n                            ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  Providers: `src/app/Providers.tsx`                              │\n│  MotionPreferenceProvider → MotionFeatures → ToastProvider       │\n└───────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────┘\n `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` │\n│ via `src/content/index`  │   │ `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`         │\n│                          │   │ `src/lib/theme.ts`              │\n└──────────────────────────┘          │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n## Component Responsibilities\n\n| Component | Responsibility | File |\n|-----------|----------------|------|\n| Bootstrap | Mount React root in StrictMode, load font and global CSS | `src/main.tsx` |\n| App | Wrap `Page` in `Providers` | `src/app/App.tsx` |\n| Providers | Compose motion preference, motion feature loading, and toast context | `src/app/Providers.tsx` |\n| Page | Derive email and other links from content; choose which sections render, in order | `src/app/Page.tsx` |\n| Content index | Single export object `siteContent` for all typed site data | `src/content/index.ts` |\n| Content schema | TypeScript types for every content record (`Profile`, `Project`, `ContactLink`, `ImageAsset`, ...) | `src/content/schema.ts` |\n| Scroll-spy | Pick the active section for nav highlighting; lock indicator during click scroll; deep-link jump | `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` |\n| Theme store | Read/write `html[data-theme]`, persist choice, follow OS and other tabs | `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/hooks/useTheme.ts` |\n| Toast | One toast at a time, polite live region, 4 s auto-hide | `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `src/components/ui/useToast.ts` |\n| Motion layer | Reduced-motion preference, feature loading, springs and easing tokens | `src/anim/index.ts`, `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `src/anim/tokens.ts` |\n| Layout chrome | Header, floating pill nav, footer, skip link, section frame | `src/layout/` |\n| Project crate and dialog | Horizontal snap row of project sleeves; native `<dialog>` for details | `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`, `src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx` |\n| Build-time gates | Budgets, colour and contrast, path case, phone-number check, image check | `scripts/*.mjs` |\n\n## Pattern Overview\n\n**Overall:** Single-page static React application. Typed, in-repo content feeds a composition root; there is no router, no server, and no runtime data fetching.\n\n**Key Characteristics:**\n- Content is code: every piece of copy, project, and link is a typed TypeScript module in `src/content/`, validated by `satisfies` clauses (for example `src/content/contacts.ts` uses `as const satisfies readonly ContactLink[]`)\n- One page with anchor sections (`SECTION_IDS` in `src/layout/nav-items.ts`); section order is fixed in `src/app/Page.tsx`\n- Theme state lives on the DOM (`html[data-theme]`), not in React state. Components subscribe with `useSyncExternalStore` through `useTheme` in `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`\n- Two animation systems are configured from one preference source. `MotionConfig` (motion) and `Globals.assign` (react-spring) both read `useMotionPreference`\n- Styling is Tailwind utilities plus named semantic layers in `src/styles/` (for example `band-invert` for the inverted Projects band, `cta` classes in `interactions.css`)\n\n## Layers\n\n**Shell (static HTML):**\n- Purpose: First paint, theme bootstrap before React, and the not-found page\n- Location: `index.html`, `404.html`\n- Contains: meta tags, inline `<script>` that sets `data-theme` from `localStorage` or `prefers-color-scheme`, static copy for the hero and section headings\n- Depends on: `public/favicon.svg`, `public/resume.pdf` via `%BASE_URL%`\n- Used by: Vite build (both are rollup inputs in `vite.config.ts`); GitHub Pages serves `404.html` for unknown paths\n\n**App composition:**\n- Purpose: Mount React and order the page\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`, `src/app/`\n- Contains: `App`, `Providers`, `Page`\n- Depends on: `src/content/index.ts`, `src/anim/index.ts`, `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`, `src/layout/*`, `src/sections/*`\n- Used by: Nothing else; it is the root\n\n**Content:**\n- Purpose: The site's data as typed modules\n- Location: `src/content/`\n- Contains: `profile.ts`, `experience.ts`, `projects.ts`, `skills.ts`, `education.ts`, `contacts.ts`, `images.ts`, `schema.ts`, `index.ts`\n- Depends on: `src/assets/images/` (via `images.ts`), `schema.ts` types\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx` and, through props, the sections\n\n**Sections:**\n- Purpose: One feature per page section, each receiving its data as props\n- Location: `src/sections/{Hero,Experience,Projects,About,Skills,Education,Contact}/`\n- Contains: `*.tsx` components; `Projects/` also has `Sleeve.tsx`, `ProjectDialog.tsx`, `project-types.ts`\n- Depends on: `src/layout/Section.tsx`, `src/components/ui/*`, `src/anim/*`, `src/lib/*`, `src/content/*` types\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`\n\n**Layout chrome:**\n- Purpose: Persistent navigation and page frame\n- Location: `src/layout/`\n- Contains: `SiteHeader.tsx` (desktop nav), `PillNav.tsx` (phone nav and pill), `Footer.tsx`, `Section.tsx`, `Container.tsx`, `SkipLink.tsx`, `Wordmark.tsx`, `nav-items.ts`\n- Depends on: `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` (passed `activeId` and `onNavigate` as props from `Page`), `src/anim/*`\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`, sections\n\n**Hooks and stores:**\n- Purpose: Shared behaviour outside React rendering\n- Location: `src/hooks/`, `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`\n- Contains: `useActiveSection`, `useTheme`, theme read/write/subscribe functions\n- Depends on: DOM APIs (`IntersectionObserver`, `MutationObserver`, `matchMedia`, `localStorage`)\n- Used by: `Page.tsx`, `ThemeToggle`, `useCopyEmail`\n\n**UI primitives:**\n- Purpose: Reusable, low-level controls and icons\n- Location: `src/components/ui/`, `src/components/motion-primitives/`\n- Contains: `ThemeToggle.tsx`, `CtaLink.tsx`, `Toast.tsx`, `useToast.ts`, `useCopyEmail.ts`, `icons/*`, `text-effect.tsx`\n- Depends on: `src/anim/*`, `src/lib/*`\n- Used by: sections and layout\n\n**Animation layer:**\n- Purpose: Motion components, feature loading, and tokens\n- Location: `src/anim/`\n- Contains: `MotionFeatures.tsx` and `featuresStatus.ts` (feature loading state), `MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `useMotionPreference.ts`, `tokens.ts`, `declarative/` (`TextReveal`, `ToastPresence`, `NavIndicator`, `PillMorph`), `physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`, `features.ts`\n- Depends on: `motion/react`, `@react-spring/web`\n- Used by: `Providers`, layout, sections, UI primitives\n\n**Utilities:**\n- Purpose: Pure helpers\n- Location: `src/lib/`\n- Contains: `utils.ts` (`cn`), `copy.ts` (`openMail` mail-client fallback and copy messages), `theme.ts`\n\n## Data Flow\n\n### Primary Request Path (first load)\n\n1. Browser requests the built `index.html`. The inline script sets `html[data-theme]` before any paint (`index.html`)\n2. Module `src/main.tsx` imports the font CSS and `src/styles/index.css`, then calls `createRoot(...).render(<StrictMode><App/></StrictMode>)`\n3. `App` renders `Providers`, then `Page` (`src/app/App.tsx`)\n4. `Page` reads `siteContent` from `src/content/index.ts` and computes `email` and `otherLinks` from `contacts` (`src/app/Page.tsx`)\n5. `Page` calls `useActiveSection(SECTION_IDS)`; the hook also performs a one-time jump for `#section` deep links (`src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`)\n6. `Page` renders sections in fixed order, passing content as props, then `Footer` and `PillNav`\n\n### Scroll and Navigation\n\n1. An `IntersectionObserver` watches each section element with a band below the sticky nav (`src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`)\n2. `pickActive` chooses the last section in page order that intersects the band; at page bottom the last section wins\n3. Clicking a nav item calls `lockTo(id)`, which sets the active id immediately and suspends observer updates until `scrollend` or a fallback timer\n4. `SiteHeader` and `PillNav` receive `activeId` and `onNavigate` as props from `Page`\n\n### Theme Toggle\n\n1. `ThemeToggle` calls `useTheme().toggle` (`src/hooks/useTheme.ts`)\n2. `applyTheme` writes `html[data-theme]` first, then persists to `localStorage` key `theme` (`src/lib/theme.ts`)\n3. `useSyncExternalStore` re-reads the attribute via `readTheme`; `MutationObserver` in `subscribeTheme` also catches attribute changes from other sources\n4. Other tabs: `storage` events re-sync the attribute. OS scheme changes apply only when nothing is stored\n\n### Project Dialog\n\n1. `Projects` holds `openIndex` state and passes `onOpen` to each `Sleeve` (`src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`)\n2. `ProjectDialog` renders a native `<dialog>` after the inverted section, so it keeps the page theme (`src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx`)\n\n**State Management:**\n- Local component state with `useState` for dialog and toast state\n- DOM as state for theme (`html[data-theme]`)\n- Static content is module-level constants, not state\n\n## Key Abstractions\n\n**Content record types:**\n- Purpose: Typed shape for each content domain\n- Examples: `src/content/schema.ts` (`Profile`, `Project`, `ExperienceEntry`, `EducationEntry`, `SkillGroup`, `ContactLink`, `ImageAsset`, `DateRange`, `Writeup`)\n- Pattern: Interfaces in `schema.ts`; data files use `satisfies` to check conformance\n\n**ImageAsset:**\n- Purpose: Image metadata plus AVIF and WebP sources for `<picture>`\n- Examples: `src/content/images.ts` (`aboutPhoto`, `capitalOneLogo`, `spectrumLogo`, `symboticLogo`)\n- Pattern: Built from `vite-imagetools` imports with `?w=...&format=avif;webp&as=picture`\n\n**Section frame:**\n- Purpose: Consistent heading, spacing, and optional aside for each section\n- Examples: `src/layout/Section.tsx`, used by `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`\n- Pattern: Section components pass `id`, `title`, `mood`, `className`, and `aside` props\n\n**Toast context:**\n- Purpose: Let any component show a short message\n- Examples: `src/components/ui/useToast.ts` (context and `useToast`), `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx` (provider)\n- Pattern: Context with a stable `show(message)` function; default context is a no-op\n\n**Theme store:**\n- Purpose: One source of truth for light or dark\n- Examples: `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`\n- Pattern: External store on `html[data-theme]`, consumed with `useSyncExternalStore`\n\n## Entry Points\n\n**Application:**\n- Location: `index.html` loads `/src/main.tsx`\n- Triggers: Any navigation to the site root or a deep link such as `#projects`\n- Responsibilities: Theme bootstrap, React mount\n\n**Not-found page:**\n- Location: `404.html` (second Vite input)\n- Triggers: GitHub Pages serves it for unknown paths\n- Responsibilities: Static \"not part of this site\" page with the same theme bootstrap\n\n**Dev and build:**\n- `vite` (dev server) and `vite build` (`dist/`), configured in `vite.config.ts`\n- Vitest entry: `vitest.config.ts` runs `tests/unit/**`; Playwright entry: `playwright.config.ts` runs `tests/e2e/**`\n\n## Architectural Constraints\n\n- **Threading:** Single-threaded browser main thread. No Web Workers were found in `src/`\n- **Global state:** Module-level `unsavedChoice` flag in `src/lib/theme.ts`; module-level `loaded` in `src/anim/featuresStatus.ts`; global react-spring `Globals` mutated by `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`\n- **Circular imports:** None detected in the paths reviewed\n- **Base path:** Every asset URL must go through `import.meta.env.BASE_URL` or `%BASE_URL%`. `vite.config.ts` appends a trailing slash to `VITE_BASE` because Vite does not add one\n- **Duplicated bootstrap:** The theme inline script is copied in `index.html` and `404.html`. The storage key `'theme'` is a literal there and a constant in `src/lib/theme.ts`\n- **rootMargin in JS:** `useActiveSection` builds `rootMargin` in code because CSS `var()` and `calc()` are not accepted by IntersectionObserver\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n### Hard-coded contact details in the static shell\n\n**What happens:** `index.html` and `404.html` contain literal copy, including the `mailto:` address and resume path in the Hero, separately from `src/content/*`.\n**Why it's wrong:** Changing `src/content/contacts.ts` or `src/content/profile.ts` does not update the first-paint markup, so the static HTML and the React page can disagree until hydration replaces the root.\n**Do this instead:** Keep copy changes in `src/content/`, and when the static shell must match, update `index.html` in the same change. `tests/unit/hero-parity.test.tsx` exists to check hero parity; extend that test rather than adding a new copy source.\n\n### Email derived by prefix slicing\n\n**What happens:** `src/app/Page.tsx` finds the `kind: 'email'` contact and strips the `mailto:` prefix with `slice(MAILTO.length)`.\n**Why it's wrong:** The address format is encoded in two places: the content `href` and the slice length. A contact with a different scheme would produce a wrong address silently.\n**Do this instead:** Store the address as a separate content field or derive it with a parser that checks the scheme before use.\n\n## Error Handling\n\n**Strategy:** Defensive, non-throwing browser API use. Storage and media-query calls are wrapped in `try/catch` and fall back to in-memory or default behaviour.\n\n**Patterns:**\n- `applyTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts` keeps the choice in memory when `localStorage` throws\n- `readTheme` / `systemTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts` return `'light'` when `matchMedia` is unavailable\n- `hashTarget` in `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` returns `null` for undecodable or unknown hashes\n- `useCopyEmail` in `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts` calls `openMail` from `src/lib/copy.ts` when the clipboard write fails, and shows a toast message\n- No React error boundary was found in `src/`\n\n## Cross-Cutting Concerns\n\n**Logging:** No `console` calls were found in `src/`\n**Validation:** Compile-time only, via TypeScript types and `satisfies` in `src/content/`; build-time checks in `scripts/*.mjs`\n**Authentication:** Not applicable\n\n---\n\n*Architecture analysis: 2026-11-19*\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":700,"cache_read_input_tokens":95302,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":700,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_014u2UY2M6z3XSuqGC4empsM","agent_id":"a96f7ac8c76e47557","session_id":"98e63eee-0876-4645-936f-e3b5a8ce91b3","uuid":"e8bb5904-8eac-4fb6-97e8-a8346e767bcc","timestamp":"2026-11-20T03:28:29.595Z","request_id":"req_011ChDUCTncghvCNVuhe9Wqw","subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Scan codebase tech and arch"}

Preference, feature loading, timing tokens, reusable animated components\n- Contains: `index.ts` (barrel for the public motion API), `MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `useMotionPreference.ts`, `MotionFeatures.tsx`, `featuresStatus.ts`, `features.ts`, `tokens.ts`, `declarative/` (`TextReveal`, `ToastPresence`, `NavIndicator`, `PillMorph`), `physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`\n- Key files: `src/anim/index.ts`, `src/anim/tokens.ts`\n\n**`src/components/`:**\n- Purpose: Reusable presentational pieces that are not page sections\n- Contains: `ui/` (controls, icons, toast system), `motion-primitives/` (`text-effect.tsx`)\n- Key files: `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx`, `src/components/ui/CtaLink.tsx`\n\n**`src/content/`:**\n- Purpose: All visible copy and data. Editing the site's words or projects happens here\n- Contains: one module per domain, plus `schema.ts` (types) and `index.ts` (exports `siteContent`)\n- Key files: `src/content/index.ts`, `src/content/schema.ts`, `src/content/images.ts`, `src/content/contacts.ts`, `src/content/projects.ts`\n\n**`src/sections/`:**\n- Purpose: One folder per page section\n- Contains: `Hero/Hero.tsx`, `Experience/Experience.tsx`, `Projects/` (`Projects.tsx`, `Sleeve.tsx`, `ProjectDialog.tsx`, `project-types.ts`), `About/About.tsx`, `Skills/Skills.tsx`, `Education/Education.tsx`, `Contact/Contact.tsx`\n- Key files: `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`\n\n**`src/layout/`:**\n- Purpose: Page frame and navigation chrome\n- Contains: `SiteHeader.tsx`, `PillNav.tsx`, `Footer.tsx`, `Section.tsx`, `Container.tsx`, `SkipLink.tsx`, `Wordmark.tsx`, `nav-items.ts`\n- Key files: `src/layout/nav-items.ts` (`SECTION_IDS`, `NAV_ITEMS`)\n\n**`src/hooks/`:**\n- Purpose: React hooks for shared behaviour\n- Contains: `useActiveSection.ts` (scroll-spy, exports `pickActive`), `useTheme.ts`\n- Key files: `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`\n\n**`src/lib/`:**\n- Purpose: Framework-free helpers\n- Contains: `utils.ts` (`cn`), `copy.ts` (`openMail` and copy messages), `theme.ts` (`readTheme`, `applyTheme`, `subscribeTheme`, `THEME_STORAGE_KEY`)\n- Key files: `src/lib/theme.ts`\n\n**`src/styles/`:**\n- Purpose: Global CSS. `index.css` is the Tailwind entry and imports the other files\n- Contains: `tokens.css` (design tokens), `base.css`, `interactions.css` (CTA, dialog, band styles), `not-found.css`\n- Key files: `src/styles/index.css`, `src/styles/tokens.css`\n\n**`tests/unit/`:**\n- Purpose: Fast unit and component tests, run by `vitest run`\n- Contains: `*.test.ts` and `*.test.tsx` covering content, theme, layout, tokens, active section, motion preference, copy, gates\n- Key files: `tests/unit/content.test.ts`, `tests/unit/hero-parity.test.tsx`, `tests/unit/theme.test.ts`\n\n**`tests/e2e/`:**\n- Purpose: Browser tests against the built site\n- Contains: `*.spec.ts` (smoke, nav, hero, theme, motion, contact, copy-email, resume, not-found, wordmark, content), `global-setup.ts`, `mobile/`\n- Key files: `tests/e2e/smoke.spec.ts`, `tests/e2e/global-setup.ts`\n\n**`scripts/`:**\n- Purpose: Build-time gates invoked by `npm run verify` and `check:*`\n- Contains: `check-js-budget.mjs`, `check-colors.mjs`, `check-contrast.mjs`, `check-path-case.mjs`, `check-no-phone.mjs`, `check-images.mjs`, `check-qa-shots.mjs`, `qa-extra-shots.mjs`, `lib/color.mjs`, `lib/tokens.mjs`, `contrast-pairs.json`\n- Key files: `scripts/check-js-budget.mjs`, `scripts/lib/tokens.mjs`\n\n**`docs/`:**\n- Purpose: Human documentation\n- Contains: `architecture.md`, `deploy-runbook.md`, `contrast-matrix.md`, `adr/` (numbered ADRs `0001` to `0010` plus `README.md`)\n- Key files: `docs/adr/README.md`\n\n**`public/`:**\n- Purpose: Static files served at the site root (with `BASE_URL` prefix)\n- Contains: `resume.pdf`, `favicon.svg`\n- Key files: `public/resume.pdf`\n\n## Key File Locations\n\n**Entry Points:**\n- `index.html`: main HTML shell and theme bootstrap script\n- `404.html`: not-found shell\n- `src/main.tsx`: React mount and global CSS and font import\n- `src/app/App.tsx`: application root component\n\n**Configuration:**\n- `vite.config.ts`: base path, plugins, `@` alias, build inputs\n- `vitest.config.ts`: unit test environment and include pattern\n- `playwright.config.ts`: e2e config\n- `tsconfig.json`, `tsconfig.app.json`, `tsconfig.node.json`, `tsconfig.test.json`, `tsconfig.scripts.json`: type checking per tree\n- `prettier.config.mjs`, `.prettierignore`, `.oxlintrc.json`, `.oxlintignore`: formatting and lint\n- `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`: CI and Pages deploy\n\n**Core Logic:**\n- `src/app/Page.tsx`: section composition\n- `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`: scroll-spy\n- `src/lib/theme.ts`: theme store\n- `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`: project crate\n\n**Testing:**\n- `tests/unit/`: Vitest specs\n- `tests/e2e/`: Playwright specs\n- `tests/fixtures/`: fixtures by area\n- `tests/setup.ts`: test setup\n\n## Naming Conventions\n\n**Files:**\n- React components: PascalCase `.tsx`, one exported component per file, for example `src/layout/SiteHeader.tsx`, `src/components/ui/CtaLink.tsx`\n- Section folders: PascalCase directory with a same-named main file, for example `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`\n- Hooks: `use` prefix, camelCase `.ts`, for example `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`, `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`\n- Icons: `*Icon.tsx` under `src/components/ui/icons/`\n- Content modules: lowercase single word `.ts`, for example `src/content/education.ts`\n- Multi-word non-component modules: kebab-case, for example `src/layout/nav-items.ts`, `src/sections/Projects/project-types.ts`\n- Lib modules: lowercase `.ts`, for example `src/lib/copy.ts`\n- Unit tests: `*.test.ts` / `*.test.tsx` in `tests/unit/`, kebab-case names, for example `tests/unit/motion-preference.test.tsx`\n- E2E tests: `*.spec.ts` in `tests/e2e/`\n- Gate scripts: `check-*.mjs` in `scripts/`\n- ADRs: `NNNN-kebab-title.md` in `docs/adr/`\n\n**Directories:**\n- `src/` subtrees: lowercase or kebab-case (`motion-primitives`, `developer-tools` appears only as a data id, not a folder)\n- Section folders under `src/sections/`: PascalCase\n\n**Identifiers in No database client or ORM is present in `package.json`\n\n**File Storage:**\n- Local filesystem only, at build time. Images are imported from `src/assets/images/` and transformed by `vite-imagetools` (see `src/content/images.ts`). Output is bundled into `dist/`\n- Static files under `public/` (`resume.pdf`, `favicon.svg`) are copied as-is\n\n**Browser storage (client-side only):**\n- `localStorage` key `theme` (constant `THEME_STORAGE_KEY` in `src/lib/theme.ts`) records an explicit light/dark choice\n- The same key is read before first paint by the inline script in `index.html` and `404.html`, so the two must stay in sync with `src/lib/theme.ts`\n- If storage is blocked, `applyTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts` catches the error and keeps the choice in memory for the page session only\n\n**Caching:**\n- None at the application level. Caching is whatever GitHub Pages applies to the static assets\n\n## Authentication & Identity\n\n**Auth Provider:**\n- None. The site has no user accounts or protected routes\n\n**Browser-level inputs read for behaviour (not identity):**\n- `prefers-color-scheme` media query, read in `src/lib/theme.ts` and the inline script in `index.html`\n- `prefers-reduced-motion`, read by `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts` and fed to both animation libraries in `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`\n- `storage` events in other tabs, subscribed in `subscribeTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts`\n\n## Monitoring & Observability\n\n**Error Tracking:**\n- None detected\n\n**Analytics:**\n- None detected. This is a build-time dependency only and is not part of the shipped bundle\n\n## Environment Configuration\n\n**Required env vars:**\n- None required to run locally\n- `VITE_BASE` (optional) - base path for asset URLs. No server endpoints exist in the repo\n\n**Outgoing:**\n- None. Overview\n\n```text\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                 Static HTML shells (build entries)               │\n│     `index.html` (main)          │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n## Component Responsibilities\n\n| Component | Responsibility | File |\n|-----------|----------------|------|\n| Bootstrap | Mount React root in StrictMode, load font and global CSS | `src/main.tsx` |\n| App | Wrap `Page` in `Providers` | `src/app/App.tsx` |\n| Providers | Compose motion preference, motion feature loading, and toast context | `src/app/Providers.tsx` |\n| Page | Derive email and other links from content; choose which sections render, in order | `src/app/Page.tsx` |\n| Content index | Single export object `siteContent` for all typed site data | `src/content/index.ts` |\n| Content schema | TypeScript types for every content record (`Profile`, `Project`, `ContactLink`, `ImageAsset`, ...) | `src/content/schema.ts` |\n| Scroll-spy | Pick the active section for nav highlighting; lock indicator during click scroll; deep-link jump | `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` |\n| Theme store | Read/write `html[data-theme]`, persist choice, follow OS and other tabs | `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/hooks/useTheme.ts` |\n| Toast | One toast at a time, polite live region, 4 s auto-hide | `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `src/components/ui/useToast.ts` |\n| Motion layer | Reduced-motion preference, feature loading, springs and easing tokens | `src/anim/index.ts`, `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `src/anim/tokens.ts` |\n| Layout chrome | Header, floating pill nav, footer, skip link, section frame | `src/layout/` |\n| Project crate and dialog | Horizontal snap row of project sleeves; native `<dialog>` for details | `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`, `src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx` |\n| Build-time gates | Budgets, colour and contrast, path case, phone-number check, image check | `scripts/*.mjs` |\n\n## Pattern Overview\n\n**Overall:** Single-page static React application. Typed, in-repo content feeds a composition root; there is no router, no server, and no runtime data fetching.\n\n**Key Characteristics:**\n- Content is code: every piece of copy, project, and link is a typed TypeScript module in `src/content/`, validated by `satisfies` clauses (for example `src/content/contacts.ts` uses `as const satisfies readonly ContactLink[]`)\n- One page with anchor sections (`SECTION_IDS` in `src/layout/nav-items.ts`); section order is fixed in `src/app/Page.tsx`\n- Theme state lives on the DOM (`html[data-theme]`), not in React state. Components subscribe with `useSyncExternalStore` through `useTheme` in `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`\n- Two animation systems are configured from one preference source. `MotionConfig` (motion) and `Globals.assign` (react-spring) both read `useMotionPreference`\n- Styling is Tailwind utilities plus named semantic layers in `src/styles/` (for example `band-invert` for the inverted Projects band, `cta` classes in `interactions.css`)\n\n## Layers\n\n**Shell (static HTML):**\n- Purpose: First paint, theme bootstrap before React, and the not-found page\n- Location: `index.html`, `404.html`\n- Contains: meta tags, inline `<script>` that sets `data-theme` from `localStorage` or `prefers-color-scheme`, static copy for the hero and section headings\n- Depends on: `public/favicon.svg`, `public/resume.pdf` via `%BASE_URL%`\n- Used by: Vite build (both are rollup inputs in `vite.config.ts`); GitHub Pages serves `404.html` for unknown paths\n\n**App composition:**\n- Purpose: Mount React and order the page\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`, `src/app/`\n- Contains: `App`, `Providers`, `Page`\n- Depends on: `src/content/index.ts`, `src/anim/index.ts`, `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`, `src/layout/*`, `src/sections/*`\n- Used by: Nothing else; it is the root\n\n**Content:**\n- Purpose: The site's data as typed modules\n- Location: `src/content/`\n- Contains: `profile.ts`, `experience.ts`, `projects.ts`, `skills.ts`, `education.ts`, `contacts.ts`, `images.ts`, `schema.ts`, `index.ts`\n- Depends on: `src/assets/images/` (via `images.ts`), `schema.ts` types\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx` and, through props, the sections\n\n**Sections:**\n- Purpose: One feature per page section, each receiving its data as props\n- Location: `src/sections/{Hero,Experience,Projects,About,Skills,Education,Contact}/`\n- Contains: `*.tsx` components; `Projects/` also has `Sleeve.tsx`, `ProjectDialog.tsx`, `project-types.ts`\n- Depends on: `src/layout/Section.tsx`, `src/components/ui/*`, `src/anim/*`, `src/lib/*`, `src/content/*` types\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`\n\n**Layout chrome:**\n- Purpose: Persistent navigation and page frame\n- Location: `src/layout/`\n- Contains: `SiteHeader.tsx` (desktop nav), `PillNav.tsx` (phone nav and pill), `Footer.tsx`, `Section.tsx`, `Container.tsx`, `SkipLink.tsx`, `Wordmark.tsx`, `nav-items.ts`\n- Depends on: `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` (passed `activeId` and `onNavigate` as props from `Page`), `src/anim/*`\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`, sections\n\n**Hooks and stores:**\n- Purpose: Shared behaviour outside React rendering\n- Location: `src/hooks/`, `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`\n- Contains: `useActiveSection`, `useTheme`, theme read/write/subscribe functions\n- Depends on: DOM APIs (`IntersectionObserver`, `MutationObserver`, `matchMedia`, `localStorage`)\n- Used by: `Page.tsx`, `ThemeToggle`, `useCopyEmail`\n\n**UI primitives:**\n- Purpose: Reusable, low-level controls and icons\n- Location: `src/components/ui/`, `src/components/motion-primitives/`\n- Contains: `ThemeToggle.tsx`, `CtaLink.tsx`, `Toast.tsx`, `useToast.ts`, `useCopyEmail.ts`, `icons/*`, `text-effect.tsx`\n- Depends on: `src/anim/*`, `src/lib/*`\n- Used by: sections and layout\n\n**Animation layer:**\n- Purpose: Motion components, feature loading, and tokens\n- Location: `src/anim/`\n- Contains: `MotionFeatures.tsx` and `featuresStatus.ts` (feature loading state), `MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `useMotionPreference.ts`, `tokens.ts`, `declarative/` (`TextReveal`, `ToastPresence`, `NavIndicator`, `PillMorph`), `physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`, `features.ts`\n- Depends on: `motion/react`, `@react-spring/web`\n- Used by: `Providers`, layout, sections, UI primitives\n\n**Utilities:**\n- Purpose: Pure helpers\n- Location: `src/lib/`\n- Contains: `utils.ts` (`cn`), `copy.ts` (`openMail` mail-client fallback and copy messages), `theme.ts`\n\n## Data Flow\n\n### Primary Request Path (first load)\n\n1. Browser requests the built `index.html`. The inline script sets `html[data-theme]` before any paint (`index.html`)\n2. Module `src/main.tsx` imports the font CSS and `src/styles/index.css`, then calls `createRoot(...).render(<StrictMode><App/></StrictMode>)`\n3. `App` renders `Providers`, then `Page` (`src/app/App.tsx`)\n4. `Page` reads `siteContent` from `src/content/index.ts` and computes `email` and `otherLinks` from `contacts` (`src/app/Page.tsx`)\n5. `Page` calls `useActiveSection(SECTION_IDS)`; the hook also performs a one-time jump for `#section` deep links (`src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`)\n6. `Page` renders sections in fixed order, passing content as props, then `Footer` and `PillNav`\n\n### Scroll and Navigation\n\n1. An `IntersectionObserver` watches each section element with a band below the sticky nav (`src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`)\n2. `pickActive` chooses the last section in page order that intersects the band; at page bottom the last section wins\n3. Clicking a nav item calls `lockTo(id)`, which sets the active id immediately and suspends observer updates until `scrollend` or a fallback timer\n4. `SiteHeader` and `PillNav` receive `activeId` and `onNavigate` as props from `Page`\n\n### Theme Toggle\n\n1. `ThemeToggle` calls `useTheme().toggle` (`src/hooks/useTheme.ts`)\n2. `applyTheme` writes `html[data-theme]` first, then persists to `localStorage` key `theme` (`src/lib/theme.ts`)\n3. `useSyncExternalStore` re-reads the attribute via `readTheme`; `MutationObserver` in `subscribeTheme` also catches attribute changes from other sources\n4. Other tabs: `storage` events re-sync the attribute. OS scheme changes apply only when nothing is stored\n\n### Project Dialog\n\n1. `Projects` holds `openIndex` state and passes `onOpen` to each `Sleeve` (`src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`)\n2. `ProjectDialog` renders a native `<dialog>` after the inverted section, so it keeps the page theme (`src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx`)\n\n**State Management:**\n- Local component state with `useState` for dialog and toast state\n- DOM as state for theme (`html[data-theme]`)\n- Static content is module-level constants, not state\n\n## Key Abstractions\n\n**Content record types:**\n- Purpose: Typed shape for each content domain\n- Examples: `src/content/schema.ts` (`Profile`, `Project`, `ExperienceEntry`, `EducationEntry`, `SkillGroup`, `ContactLink`, `ImageAsset`, `DateRange`, `Writeup`)\n- Pattern: Interfaces in `schema.ts`; data files use `satisfies` to check conformance\n\n**ImageAsset:**\n- Purpose: Image metadata plus AVIF and WebP sources for `<picture>`\n- Examples: `src/content/images.ts` (`aboutPhoto`, `capitalOneLogo`, `spectrumLogo`, `symboticLogo`)\n- Pattern: Built from `vite-imagetools` imports with `?w=...&format=avif;webp&as=picture`\n\n**Section frame:**\n- Purpose: Consistent heading, spacing, and optional aside for each section\n- Examples: `src/layout/Section.tsx`, used by `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`\n- Pattern: Section components pass `id`, `title`, `mood`, `className`, and `aside` props\n\n**Toast context:**\n- Purpose: Let any component show a short message\n- Examples: `src/components/ui/useToast.ts` (context and `useToast`), `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx` (provider)\n- Pattern: Context with a stable `show(message)` function; default context is a no-op\n\n**Theme store:**\n- Purpose: One source of truth for light or dark\n- Examples: `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`\n- Pattern: External store on `html[data-theme]`, consumed with `useSyncExternalStore`\n\n## Entry Points\n\n**Application:**\n- Location: `index.html` loads `/src/main.tsx`\n- Triggers: Any navigation to the site root or a deep link such as `#projects`\n- Responsibilities: Theme bootstrap, React mount\n\n**Not-found page:**\n- Location: `404.html` (second Vite input)\n- Triggers: GitHub Pages serves it for unknown paths\n- Responsibilities: Static \"not part of this site\" page with the same theme bootstrap\n\n**Dev and build:**\n- `vite` (dev server) and `vite build` (`dist/`), configured in `vite.config.ts`\n- Vitest entry: `vitest.config.ts` runs `tests/unit/**`; Playwright entry: `playwright.config.ts` runs `tests/e2e/**`\n\n## Architectural Constraints\n\n- **Threading:** Single-threaded browser main thread. No Web Workers were found in `src/`\n- **Global state:** Module-level `unsavedChoice` flag in `src/lib/theme.ts`; module-level `loaded` in `src/anim/featuresStatus.ts`; global react-spring `Globals` mutated by `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`\n- **Circular imports:** None detected in the paths reviewed\n- **Base path:** Every asset URL must go through `import.meta.env.BASE_URL` or `%BASE_URL%`. `vite.config.ts` appends a trailing slash to `VITE_BASE` because Vite does not add one\n- **Duplicated bootstrap:** The theme inline script is copied in `index.html` and `404.html`. The storage key `'theme'` is a literal there and a constant in `src/lib/theme.ts`\n- **rootMargin in JS:** `useActiveSection` builds `rootMargin` in code because CSS `var()` and `calc()` are not accepted by IntersectionObserver\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n### Hard-coded contact details in the static shell\n\n**What happens:** `index.html` and `404.html` contain literal copy, including the `mailto:` address and resume path in the Hero, separately from `src/content/*`.\n**Why it's wrong:** Changing `src/content/contacts.ts` or `src/content/profile.ts` does not update the first-paint markup, so the static HTML and the React page can disagree until hydration replaces the root.\n**Do this instead:** Keep copy changes in `src/content/`, and when the static shell must match, update `index.html` in the same change. `tests/unit/hero-parity.test.tsx` exists to check hero parity; extend that test rather than adding a new copy source.\n\n### Email derived by prefix slicing\n\n**What happens:** `src/app/Page.tsx` finds the `kind: 'email'` contact and strips the `mailto:` prefix with `slice(MAILTO.length)`.\n**Why it's wrong:** The address format is encoded in two places: the content `href` and the slice length. A contact with a different scheme would produce a wrong address silently.\n**Do this instead:** Store the address as a separate content field or derive it with a parser that checks the scheme before use.\n\n## Error Handling\n\n**Strategy:** Defensive, non-throwing browser API use. Storage and media-query calls are wrapped in `try/catch` and fall back to in-memory or default behaviour.\n\n**Patterns:**\n- `applyTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts` keeps the choice in memory when `localStorage` throws\n- `readTheme` / `systemTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts` return `'light'` when `matchMedia` is unavailable\n- `hashTarget` in `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` returns `null` for undecodable or unknown hashes\n- `useCopyEmail` in `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts` calls `openMail` from `src/lib/copy.ts` when the clipboard write fails, and shows a toast message\n- No React error boundary was found in `src/`\n\n## Cross-Cutting Concerns\n\n**Logging:** No `console` calls were found in `src/`\n**Validation:** Compile-time only, via TypeScript types and `satisfies` in `src/content/`; build-time checks in `scripts/*.mjs`\n**Authentication:** Not applicable\n\n---\n\n*Architecture analysis: 2026-11-19*\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1,"oldLines":5,"newStart":1,"newLines":5,"lines":[" ---","-last_mapped_commit: 2c291d0ffaea8a86329dc1e912149f66ae05532e","+last_mapped_commit: 781253a3efc402f8061fff074077a981541f4c37"," last_mapped_at: 2026-11-19"," ---"," <!-- refreshed: 2026-11-19 -->"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false,"contentNotInModelContext":true},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"toolu_012eC2hvc7FvkazGFkJjLAik","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"rule"}}]}

Tailwind entry index.css, tokens.css, base.css, interactions.css, not-found.css\n│   ├── types/              # Ambient declarations (imagetools.d.ts)\n│   └── main.tsx            # Browser entry\n├── tests/\n│   ├── unit/               # Vitest + Testing Library specs (*.test.ts / *.test.tsx)\n│   ├── e2e/                # Playwright specs (*.spec.ts), global-setup.ts, mobile/ subfolder\n│   ├── fixtures/           # Test fixtures grouped by area (anim, app, motion-primitives, sections)\n│   └── setup.ts            # Vitest setup file\n├── scripts/                # Build-verification gates (check-*.mjs), lib/ helpers, qa-extra-shots.mjs\n├── public/                 # Copied verbatim to dist/ (resume.pdf, favicon.svg)\n├── docs/                   # Local tooling config (config.json)\n├── .claude/                # Claude Code project config and CLAUDE.md\n├── index.html              # Main HTML shell (Vite entry)\n├── 404.html                # Not-found shell (second Vite entry)\n├── vite.config.ts          # Vite: base path, plugins, aliases, two-entry build\n├── vitest.config.ts        # Vitest: jsdom, tests/unit only\n├── playwright.config.ts    # Playwright e2e config\n├── tsconfig.json           # Project references; plus tsconfig.app/node/test/scripts.json\n├── package.json            # Scripts and dependencies\n└── prettier.config.mjs, .oxlintrc.json, .oxlintignore, .prettierignore, .gitignore\n```\n\n## Directory Purposes\n\n**`src/app/`:**\n- Purpose: Application root and page order\n- Contains: `App.tsx` (renders `Providers` then `Page`), `Providers.tsx` (motion and toast providers), `Page.tsx` (section order and email derivation)\n- Key files: `src/app/Page.tsx`, `src/app/Providers.tsx`\n\n**`src/anim/`:**\n- Purpose: Everything motion. Preference, feature loading, timing tokens, reusable animated components\n- Contains: `index.ts` (barrel for the public motion API), `MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `useMotionPreference.ts`, `MotionFeatures.tsx`, `featuresStatus.ts`, `features.ts`, `tokens.ts`, `declarative/` (`TextReveal`, `ToastPresence`, `NavIndicator`, `PillMorph`), `physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`\n- Key files: `src/anim/index.ts`, `src/anim/tokens.ts`\n\n**`src/components/`:**\n- Purpose: Reusable presentational pieces that are not page sections\n- Contains: `ui/` (controls, icons, toast system), `motion-primitives/` (`text-effect.tsx`)\n- Key files: `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx`, `src/components/ui/CtaLink.tsx`\n\n**`src/content/`:**\n- Purpose: All visible copy and data. Editing the site's words or projects happens here\n- Contains: one module per domain, plus `schema.ts` (types) and `index.ts` (exports `siteContent`)\n- Key files: `src/content/index.ts`, `src/content/schema.ts`, `src/content/images.ts`, `src/content/contacts.ts`, `src/content/projects.ts`\n\n**`src/sections/`:**\n- Purpose: One folder per page section\n- Contains: `Hero/Hero.tsx`, `Experience/Experience.tsx`, `Projects/` (`Projects.tsx`, `Sleeve.tsx`, `ProjectDialog.tsx`, `project-types.ts`), `About/About.tsx`, `Skills/Skills.tsx`, `Education/Education.tsx`, `Contact/Contact.tsx`\n- Key files: `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`\n\n**`src/layout/`:**\n- Purpose: Page frame and navigation chrome\n- Contains: `SiteHeader.tsx`, `PillNav.tsx`, `Footer.tsx`, `Section.tsx`, `Container.tsx`, `SkipLink.tsx`, `Wordmark.tsx`, `nav-items.ts`\n- Key files: `src/layout/nav-items.ts` (`SECTION_IDS`, `NAV_ITEMS`)\n\n**`src/hooks/`:**\n- Purpose: React hooks for shared behaviour\n- Contains: `useActiveSection.ts` (scroll-spy, exports `pickActive`), `useTheme.ts`\n- Key files: `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`\n\n**`src/lib/`:**\n- Purpose: Framework-free helpers\n- Contains: `utils.ts` (`cn`), `copy.ts` (`openMail` and copy messages), `theme.ts` (`readTheme`, `applyTheme`, `subscribeTheme`, `THEME_STORAGE_KEY`)\n- Key files: `src/lib/theme.ts`\n\n**`src/styles/`:**\n- Purpose: Global CSS. `index.css` is the Tailwind entry and imports the other files\n- Contains: `tokens.css` (design tokens), `base.css`, `interactions.css` (CTA, dialog, band styles), `not-found.css`\n- Key files: `src/styles/index.css`, `src/styles/tokens.css`\n\n**`tests/unit/`:**\n- Purpose: Fast unit and component tests, run by `vitest run`\n- Contains: `*.test.ts` and `*.test.tsx` covering content, theme, layout, tokens, active section, motion preference, copy, gates\n- Key files: `tests/unit/content.test.ts`, `tests/unit/hero-parity.test.tsx`, `tests/unit/theme.test.ts`\n\n**`tests/e2e/`:**\n- Purpose: Browser tests against the built site\n- Contains: `*.spec.ts` (smoke, nav, hero, theme, motion, contact, copy-email, resume, not-found, wordmark, content), `global-setup.ts`, `mobile/`\n- Key files: `tests/e2e/smoke.spec.ts`, `tests/e2e/global-setup.ts`\n\n**`scripts/`:**\n- Purpose: Build-time gates invoked by `npm run verify` and `check:*`\n- Contains: `check-js-budget.mjs`, `check-colors.mjs`, `check-contrast.mjs`, `check-path-case.mjs`, `check-no-phone.mjs`, `check-images.mjs`, `check-qa-shots.mjs`, `qa-extra-shots.mjs`, `lib/color.mjs`, `lib/tokens.mjs`, `contrast-pairs.json`\n- Key files: `scripts/check-js-budget.mjs`, `scripts/lib/tokens.mjs`\n\n**`docs/`:**\n- Purpose: Human documentation\n- Contains: `architecture.md`, `deploy-runbook.md`, `contrast-matrix.md`, `adr/` (numbered ADRs `0001` to `0010` plus `README.md`)\n- Key files: `docs/adr/README.md`\n\n**`public/`:**\n- Purpose: Static files served at the site root (with `BASE_URL` prefix)\n- Contains: `resume.pdf`, `favicon.svg`\n- Key files: `public/resume.pdf`\n\n## Key File Locations\n\n**Entry Points:**\n- `index.html`: main HTML shell and theme bootstrap script\n- `404.html`: not-found shell\n- `src/main.tsx`: React mount and global CSS and font import\n- `src/app/App.tsx`: application root component\n\n**Configuration:**\n- `vite.config.ts`: base path, plugins, `@` alias, build inputs\n- `vitest.config.ts`: unit test environment and include pattern\n- `playwright.config.ts`: e2e config\n- `tsconfig.json`, `tsconfig.app.json`, `tsconfig.node.json`, `tsconfig.test.json`, `tsconfig.scripts.json`: type checking per tree\n- `prettier.config.mjs`, `.prettierignore`, `.oxlintrc.json`, `.oxlintignore`: formatting and lint\n- `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`: CI and Pages deploy\n\n**Core Logic:**\n- `src/app/Page.tsx`: section composition\n- `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`: scroll-spy\n- `src/lib/theme.ts`: theme store\n- `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`: project crate\n\n**Testing:**\n- `tests/unit/`: Vitest specs\n- `tests/e2e/`: Playwright specs\n- `tests/fixtures/`: fixtures by area\n- `tests/setup.ts`: test setup\n\n## Naming Conventions\n\n**Files:**\n- React components: PascalCase `.tsx`, one exported component per file, for example `src/layout/SiteHeader.tsx`, `src/components/ui/CtaLink.tsx`\n- Section folders: PascalCase directory with a same-named main file, for example `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`\n- Hooks: `use` prefix, camelCase `.ts`, for example `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`, `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`\n- Icons: `*Icon.tsx` under `src/components/ui/icons/`\n- Content modules: lowercase single word `.ts`, for example `src/content/education.ts`\n- Multi-word non-component modules: kebab-case, for example `src/layout/nav-items.ts`, `src/sections/Projects/project-types.ts`\n- Lib modules: lowercase `.ts`, for example `src/lib/copy.ts`\n- Unit tests: `*.test.ts` / `*.test.tsx` in `tests/unit/`, kebab-case names, for example `tests/unit/motion-preference.test.tsx`\n- E2E tests: `*.spec.ts` in `tests/e2e/`\n- Gate scripts: `check-*.mjs` in `scripts/`\n- ADRs: `NNNN-kebab-title.md` in `docs/adr/`\n\n**Directories:**\n- `src/` subtrees: lowercase or kebab-case (`motion-primitives`, `developer-tools` appears only as a data id, not a folder)\n- Section folders under `src/sections/`: PascalCase\n\n**Identifiers in data:**\n- Section ids: lowercase, `SECTION_IDS` in `src/layout/nav-items.ts` (`hero`, `experience`, `projects`, `about`, `skills`, `education`, `contact`)\n- Content record ids: kebab-case strings, for example `id: 'capital-one'` in `src/content/experience.ts`, `id: 'recipecart'` in `src/content/projects.ts`\n\n## Where to Add New Code\n\n**New page section:**\n- Primary code: `src/sections/<Name>/<Name>.tsx`, rendered in `src/app/Page.tsx` at the desired position\n- Nav link (optional): add to `SECTION_IDS` and `NAV_ITEMS` in `src/layout/nav-items.ts`. Section `id` must match the `SECTION_IDS` entry\n- Data: a new module in `src/content/` exported through `src/content/index.ts` (`siteContent`), typed in `src/content/schema.ts`\n- Tests: `tests/unit/<name>.test.tsx`\n\n**New content record:**\n- Data file: existing `src/content/<domain>.ts`, using the types in `src/content/schema.ts` and `satisfies`\n- Images: place source in `src/assets/images/` and import with vite-imagetools query, following `src/content/images.ts`\n\n**New reusable UI control:**\n- Implementation: `src/components/ui/<Name>.tsx`\n- Icon: `src/components/ui/icons/<Name>Icon.tsx` with a `className?: string` prop\n\n**New animation component:**\n- Implementation: `src/anim/declarative/<Name>.tsx`, exported from `src/anim/index.ts` if public\n\n**New hook:**\n- Implementation: `src/hooks/use<Name>.ts`\n\n**New shared helper:**\n- Implementation: `src/lib/<name>.ts`\n\n**New global style or token:**\n- Tokens: `src/styles/tokens.css`;
